```python
    # ... other wired services

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SessionBootstrapState:
    config: GameConfig
    services: SessionRuntimeServices
    # ... other owned state

class SessionBootstrap:
    @staticmethod
    def _build_services(*, registries, event_log=None, ...): ...

    @staticmethod
    def new_game_state(config, *, ai_factory=None) -> SessionBootstrapState: ...

class GameSession:
    def __init__(self, config=None, ai_factory=None, *, _state=None):
        self._race_registry = None
        if _state is not None:
            self._apply_bootstrap_state(_state)
            return
        state = SessionBootstrap.new_game_state(config or GameConfig(), ai_factory=ai_factory)
        self._apply_bootstrap_state(state)

    @classmethod
    def from_dict(cls, data, ai_factory=None):
        session = cls.__new__(cls)
        session._race_registry = None
        state = SessionPersistenceAdapter.rehydrate_state(data, ai_factory=ai_factory, ...)
        session._apply_bootstrap_state(state)
        return session

    def _apply_bootstrap_state(self, state):
        # Single state-mutation site.
        self._services = state.services
        # ... other owned fields
```

Boundary: this pattern is for *structural* drift, not behavioral cleanup. If the two construction paths have intentionally different semantics (e.g. `human_player_ids` fallback differing between fresh and load paths), keep that asymmetry inside the state-builder for each path. The single-assignment method only copies from the state; it does not enforce equality across paths.

## 43. Unified Container Substrate

Where: `game/strategy/data/container.py::Container` (PROJ-436 Phase 0
substrate), `game/strategy/data/containable.py`,
`game/strategy/data/bay_inventory.py::BayInventory` (Phase 2 widened to
the four-slot Container projection), `game/strategy/engine/production_engine.py::IProductionResourceSource`
(Phase 8 polymorphic-protocol seam).

Use this pattern when a storage surface needs to hold heterogeneous
mass-priced content (resources, items, population) under one
capacity cap with one policy filter. PROJ-436 unified eight separate
storage fields and three ability variants (`ResourceStorage`,
`CargoStorage`, `VehicleBay`) into one `Container` abstraction; the
typed `BayInventory` shipped four parallel slots
(`bay: list[CarriedVehicle]`, `pods: list[DropPod]`,
`resources: dict[str, float]`, `population: dict[str, int]`) sharing
one mass cap.

Contract:

- `Container(capacity_mass, policy, resources, items, population)`
  carries three internal slices. Mass is the universal capacity gate
  â€” resource mass-per-unit resolves through
  `ResourceCatalog.get_mass_per_unit()`; item mass comes from the
  `ItemRef`; population mass comes from `species_mass_per_unit()`.
- `ContainerPolicy(allowed_kinds, allowed_type_ids=None)` filters by
  `ContainableKind` (RESOURCE / ITEM / POPULATION) plus an optional
  type-id allowlist. Policy is data, not code â€” a `metals_silo` with
  `allowed_type_ids=["metals"]` and a generic cargo hold with
  `allowed_type_ids=None` use the same engine path.
- `Container.accepts(containable)` is the policy seam for
  container-backed write paths and the unified projection surface
  (`BayInventory.container_view()` is a snapshot for callers that
  want unified accounting). **Transfer-kind validation does NOT go
  through `Container.accepts()` today** â€” `TransferValidator._is_known_cargo_type`
  routes through `ResourceCatalog.has()` plus three categorical
  sentinels (`passengers`, `drop_pod`, `vehicle`) directly; a future
  pass could wire the validator through `Container.accepts()` if a
  per-container policy check is needed, but that is not the current
  contract.
- For the production-engine read/consume path, the
  `IProductionResourceSource` Protocol (Phase 8) is the narrower
  polymorphic seam â€” `Planet` (over its stockpile API) and `Fleet`
  (over its cargo API) both satisfy
  `production_has_resources` / `production_get_resource` /
  `production_consume_resource`. The engine reads through these
  three methods without dispatching on entity type.
- Test-only: `set_resource_catalog(catalog)` on `container.py` injects
  a deterministic catalog for unit tests (PROJ-258
  `get_default_*` / `set_default_*` accessor convention).

Use for: any new storage surface that holds mixed mass-priced
content (drones-on-a-shipyard, planet-side civilian housing, etc.).
Reuse `Container` with a tailored `ContainerPolicy` rather than
inventing a parallel typed-list field per category. For
production-engine integration, satisfy `IProductionResourceSource`
via thin polymorphic delegators (do not extend the Protocol with
entity-specific methods).

Boundary: launch / recovery / life support / production stay
**separate** abilities â€” `Container` is storage only. Today's
**live** write substrates remain typed: `CarriedVehicle` items live
in `BayInventory.bay` (a `list[CarriedVehicle]` â€” see Pattern #38);
`DropPod` items live in `BayInventory.pods` (a `list[DropPod]`);
`Planet.stockpile` is a `dict[str, float]` over the `IStockpileHolder`
protocol; fleet cargo aggregates per-ship `_cargo_contents`
substrate through `Fleet.get_cargo_resource` /
`consume_cargo_resource` (the `BayInventory.resources` slot exists
from Phase 2 widening but production cargo has not been migrated
into it). `Container` and `Container.accepts()` are the unified
projection / policy seam over those typed substrates, not a
wholesale replacement of them â€” `BayInventory.container_view()`
returns a snapshot, not a mutable view. Deployed groups (`FighterWing`,
`SatelliteConstellation`, `MineGroup` on `empire.deployed_groups`)
are not in any container â€” Container handles only pre-deployment /
post-recovery storage. See Pattern #37 for the typed `DeployedGroup`
family.

> **Last verified:** 2026-05-18 â€” PROJ-436 Phase 10 doc refresh +
> Phase 11 consult disposition.
> Container substrate landed in Phase 0; `BayInventory` widened to
> four slots in Phase 2; `TransferValidator.VALID_CARGO_TYPES`
> deleted in Phase 7 â€” replaced by `ResourceCatalog.has()` + three
> categorical sentinels (`Container.accepts()` is the analogous
> seam for container-backed write paths but is NOT called by the
> current transfer validator);
> `ProductionEngine.context_type` storage-dispatch deleted in
> Phase 8 via the `IProductionResourceSource` Protocol;
> `_CarriedItemsProxy` deleted in Phase 9.

## Critical Naming Reminders

- Ship inherits `(PhysicsBody, ShipPhysicsMixin)` â€” there is no `ShipCombatMixin`.
- Config classes in `game/core/config.py` are plain classes, not dataclasses.
- Use `BattleScreen` / `StrategyScreen`, not `BattleScene` / `StrategyScene`.
- Use `VehicleDesignService`, not `ShipBuilderService`.
- `PolicyManager` lives at `game/ai/policy_manager.py`.
- Workshop `EventBus` lives at `game/ui/screens/builder/event_bus.py`; it is distinct from `game/core/event_logging.py::EventBus` used by simulation/strategy logging.
- Galaxy/Planet/Star read protocols (`IGalaxySystemGraph`, `IGalaxySpatialQuery`, `IHabitabilityCalculator`, `IStockpileHolder`, `IStagingYardHolder`) live at `game/strategy/data/galaxy_protocols.py`, beside the data classes; the cross-layer protocol package is `game/core/protocols/`.
- Mutator protocols (`IFleetMutator`, `IPlanetMutator`, `IEmpireMutator`, `IShipInstanceMutator`) live at `game/core/protocols/strategy_mutators.py`.

## Quick File/API Reminders

- `ApplicationContext`: `game/context.py`.
- Registry provider: `game/core/registry.py::DefaultRegistryProvider`, `TestRegistryProvider`, `get_default_registry_provider`.
- Protocols: `game/core/protocols/`.
- Strategy facade: `game/strategy/facade/strategy_session_facade.py`.
- Battle runner: `game/simulation/battle_runner.py::run_battle`.
- Battle spec/outcome DTOs: `game/simulation/battle_spec.py`, `game/simulation/battle_outcome.py`.
- Ability stat registry: `game/simulation/combat/ability_stat_registry.py`.
- Weapon registry: `game/simulation/combat/weapon_registry.py`.
- Stat contributor registry: `game/simulation/entities/stat_contributors/registry.py`.
- Universal ability source adapters: `game/strategy/services/ability_sources/`.
- Strategy modal base: `game/ui/screens/strategy_modal_window.py`.
- UI widget factory: `tests/fixtures/ui_widget_factory.py`.
- Turn config: `game/strategy/engine/turn_engine_config.py`.
- Tick phases: `game/simulation/systems/tick_phase.py`.

## Extension Checklist

- New battle launch: compile `BattleSpec`; call `run_battle` or `BattleController.start_from_spec`; pass `registry_provider` when no ship builder is supplied.
- New strategic effect source: implement `IAbilitySource`, register source provider, add owner/scope validation tests.
- New combat modifier ability: register in `ABILITY_STAT_REGISTRY`, update `KNOWN_EXTERNAL_STAT_KEYS`, add tests through quickstart complex coverage.
- New weapon family: add handler and metadata, register via family module import, keep central systems unchanged.
- New recalculation stat contributor: use `register_stat_contributor`, typed `StatAccumulator`, explicit conflict policy, and handle cleanup.
- New turn sub-engine: add dependency to `TurnEngineConfig.create_default`, add/adjust phase descriptor, validate preconditions before mutation.
- New strategy modal: subclass `StrategyModalWindow` and require keyword-only `window_manager`.
- New complex UI class: use Compositional Construction; for legacy UIWindow tests only, use scoped `bypass_init`.
- New registry consumer in simulation: inject provider/registries; never call global registry lookup.
- New random behavior in combat/AI/engine: accept and use injected `random.Random`; add determinism coverage if behavior affects outcomes.

## Guard And Test Anchors

- Registry/global lookup guard: simulation must not call `get_default_registry_provider()`.
- Mutation boundary guard: `tests/unit/strategy/data/test_mutator_boundary_ast_guard.py`.

C:/Developer/StarshipBattles/docs/systems/orders_system.md:292:`IOrderHandler.execute_for_issuer(*, issuer, order_owner, empire, galaxy=None, registries=None) -> OrderExecutionResult` is the unified contract used by external dispatchers that need to run an order against an arbitrary `IIssuerAdapter`-wrapped issuer (planet via `PlanetStagingYardIssuerAdapter`, fleet via `FleetShipIssuerAdapter`). C:/Developer/StarshipBattles/docs/systems/strategy_layer.md:309:**Issuer-aware execution contract (PROJ-438 Phase 6).** `OrderProcessor.get_handler(order_type)` is the public accessor used by external dispatchers (currently `ActionExecutionEngine._execute_planet_action`, which wraps a planet in a `PlanetStagingYardIssuerAdapter` and dispatches the planet's queued action order through the same registered `IOrderHandler` a fleet would use). `IOrderHandler.execute_for_issuer(*, issuer, order_owner, empire, galaxy=None, registries=None)` is the canonical 5-kwarg signature; launch and recovery handlers share it (recovery handlers accept and ignore `galaxy` / `registries`). 246:         self,
247:         planet: Any,

251:     ) -> Optional[ActionTickResult]:
252:         """Tick a planet's FMS action order if one is queued.
253: 
254:         Planets have no speed concept, so they act every tick (interval=1).
255:         Mirrors :meth:`_process_fleet_action_tick` for the FMS subset only;
256:         ACTIVATE/DEACTIVATE toggles stay on PlanetActionEngine.
257:         """
258:         get_current = getattr(planet, "get_current_order", None)
259:         if get_current is None:
260:             return None
261:         order = get_current()
262:         if order is None:
263:             return None
264:         if order.type not in order_metadata.planet_fms_action_order_types:
265:             return None
266: 
267:         order.execution_progress += 1
268: 
269:         if self._action_time_resolver is not None:
270:             action_time = self._action_time_resolver.resolve_action_time(
271:                 planet, order, component_registry
272:             )
273:         else:
274:             action_time = ActionTimeResolver.resolve_action_time(
275:                 planet, order, component_registry
276:             )
277: 
278:         if order.execution_progress >= action_time:
279:             self._execute_planet_action(
280:                 planet, empire, component_registry,
281:             )
282:             return ActionTickResult(
283:                 fleet_id=getattr(planet, "id", -1),
284:                 order_type=order.type,
285:                 action_completed=True,
286:                 fleet_consumed=False,
287:                 execution_progress=order.execution_progress,
288:                 action_time=action_time,
289:             )
290:         return ActionTickResult(
291:             fleet_id=getattr(planet, "id", -1),
292:             order_type=order.type,
293:             action_completed=False,
294:             fleet_consumed=False,
295:             execution_progress=order.execution_progress,
296:             action_time=action_time,
297:         )

275: class PlanetStagingYardIssuerAdapter:
276:     """IIssuerAdapter wrapping a planet's staging yard.
277: 
278:     Used when a planet's facility (with ``StrategicMineLayer`` /
279:     ``StrategicFighterLaunch`` / ``StrategicSatelliteLaunch`` /
280:     ``RecoverFighters`` / ``RecoverSatellites``) issues an FMS order.
281: 
282:     Pop/append act on :attr:`Planet.staging_yard`. ``append_recovered``
283:     routes through :meth:`Planet.add_to_staging_yard` so the
284:     ``max_staging_mass`` cap is honoured.
285:     """
286: 
287:     def __init__(self, planet: "Planet") -> None:
288:         self._planet = planet
289: 
290:     @property
291:     def planet(self) -> "Planet":
292:         return self._planet
293: 
294:     @property
295:     def location(self) -> HexCoord:
296:         # Planet.location is local system coords; FMS orders execute at
297:         # the planet's global hex. Use the planet's owner's galaxy lookup
298:         # if the planet exposes a global hex shortcut; otherwise fall
299:         # back to ``location`` (tests that wrap a free-standing planet
300:         # set ``location`` directly to the global hex).
301:         gh = getattr(self._planet, "global_hex", None)
302:         if gh is not None:
303:             return gh  # type: ignore[no-any-return]
304:         return self._planet.location
305: 
306:     @property
307:     def owner_id(self) -> int:
308:         return int(self._planet.owner_id) if self._planet.owner_id is not None else -1
309: 
310:     @property
311:     def display_label(self) -> str:
312:         name = getattr(self._planet, "name", None)
313:         if name:
314:             return f"Planet {name}"
315:         return f"Planet {getattr(self._planet, 'id', '?')}"
316: 
317:     def pop_carried(
318:         self,
319:         vehicle_type: str,
320:         design_id: Optional[str],
321:         count: Optional[int],
322:     ) -> List[Any]:
323:         yard = self._planet.staging_yard
324:         popped: List[Any] = []
325:         remaining: List[Any] = []
326:         limit = count if (count is not None and count >= 0) else None
327:         for item in yard:
328:             if limit is not None and len(popped) >= limit:
329:                 remaining.append(item)
330:                 continue
331:             if _matches(item, vehicle_type, design_id):
332:                 popped.append(item)
333:             else:
334:                 remaining.append(item)
335:         self._planet._staging_yard = remaining
336:         return popped
337: 
338:     def count_carried(
339:         self,
340:         vehicle_type: str,
341:         design_id: Optional[str],
342:     ) -> int:
343:         return sum(
344:             1 for item in self._planet.staging_yard
345:             if _matches(item, vehicle_type, design_id)
346:         )
347: 
348:     def append_carried(self, items: List[Any]) -> int:
349:         """Append items, respecting ``max_staging_mass`` if set.
350: 
351:         Honours partial-success: stops at the first item that would
352:         overflow capacity and returns the count actually appended.
353:         """
354:         appended = 0
355:         for item in items:
356:             ok = self._planet.add_to_staging_yard(item)
357:             if not ok:
358:                 break
359:             appended += 1
360:         return appended
361: 
362:     def append_recovered(self, vehicle: CarriedVehicle) -> bool:
363:         # PROJ-450 Phase 1: pass the typed CarriedVehicle directly;
364:         # Planet normalises to dict internally.
365:         return bool(self._planet.add_to_staging_yard(vehicle))

44: class _FixedActionTimeResolver:
45:     """Test double for ``ActionTimeResolver``.
46: 
47:     Returns a constant ``action_time`` so the order completes on the

50:     component-registry-driven behaviour is out of scope for this test
51:     file.

55:         self._action_time = action_time
56: 
57:     def resolve_action_time(self, _issuer, _order, _component_registry) -> int:
58:         return self._action_time

61: class _StubPlanet:
62:     """Minimal Planet shape compatible with PlanetStagingYardIssuerAdapter
63:     and the planet branch of every planet-FMS order handler.

70:         self,
71:         *,
72:         planet_id: int,
73:         owner_id: int,
74:         location: HexCoord,

76:     ) -> None:
77:         self.id = planet_id

79:         self.location = location

87:     def staging_yard(self) -> list:
88:         """PROJ-455: backed by ``_staging_yard`` to honour the
89:         :class:`PlanetStagingYardIssuerAdapter` write contract at
90:         ``issuer_adapter.py:335`` (``self._planet._staging_yard = remaining``).
91:         Real :class:`Planet` exposes the same underscore-backed property pair;
92:         the precedent stub in ``test_fms_planet_lay_mines.py`` skips this
93:         because its assertions never observe the post-pop yard state.
94:         """

98:         return self.orders[0] if self.orders else None

101:         if self.orders:
102:             return self.orders.pop(0)
103:         return None

106:         self.orders.append(order)

110:             item_mass = float(item.mass)

340: def _assert_post_dispatch_state(planet, empire, order_type: OrderType) -> None:
341:     """Per-order-type observable post-condition checks for the
342:     completion-branch parametrised test.
343: 
344:     Conservative shape: launch / lay orders must empty the staging yard
345:     and produce exactly one deployed group; recovery orders must empty
346:     the deployed group(s) of their ships and place the recovered ship
347:     into the staging yard. Whether the empty group is removed from
348:     ``empire.deployed_groups`` is handler-policy-defined; the assertions
349:     only require the ships count = 0 on any remaining groups.
350:     """
351:     if order_type is OrderType.LAY_MINES:
352:         mine_groups = [
353:             g for g in empire.deployed_groups
354:             if g.__class__.__name__ == "MineGroup"
355:         ]
356:         assert len(mine_groups) == 1, (
357:             "LAY_MINES should produce exactly one MineGroup; "
358:             f"got {len(mine_groups)}."
359:         )
360:         assert len(planet.staging_yard) == 0, (
361:             "Mine should have moved out of the staging yard."
362:         )
363:     elif order_type in (OrderType.LAUNCH_FIGHTERS, OrderType.LAUNCH_SATELLITES):
364:         assert len(planet.staging_yard) == 0, (
365:             f"{order_type.name} should have emptied the staging yard."
366:         )
367:         assert len(empire.deployed_groups) == 1, (
368:             f"{order_type.name} should produce exactly one deployed group; "
369:             f"got {len(empire.deployed_groups)}."
370:         )
371:     elif order_type in (OrderType.RECOVER_FIGHTERS, OrderType.RECOVER_SATELLITES):
372:         for group in empire.deployed_groups:
373:             assert len(group.ships) == 0, (
374:                 f"{order_type.name} should empty the deployed group's "
375:                 f"ships; got {len(group.ships)} remaining."
376:             )
377:         assert len(planet.staging_yard) >= 1, (
378:             f"{order_type.name} should have placed the recovered ship "
379:             f"into the staging yard."
380:         )
381:     else:
382:         pytest.fail(
383:             f"Unhandled order_type in _assert_post_dispatch_state: "
384:             f"{order_type!r}"
385:         )
386: 
387: 
388: @pytest.mark.parametrize(
389:     "order_type",
390:     [
391:         OrderType.LAY_MINES,
392:         OrderType.LAUNCH_FIGHTERS,
393:         OrderType.LAUNCH_SATELLITES,
394:         OrderType.RECOVER_FIGHTERS,
395:         OrderType.RECOVER_SATELLITES,
396:     ],
397: )

248:     def _create_fighter_group(
249:         self, *, empire: "Empire", target_hex: HexCoord,
250:     ) -> FighterWing:
251:         """Mint a new :class:`FighterWing` for this launch action.
252: 
253:         PROJ-431 Phase 3: a deployed fighter wing is a sibling of
254:         :class:`Fleet`, not a Fleet. The wing lands on
255:         ``empire.deployed_groups`` and the fleet-action surface (Move,
256:         Warp, Build, Join) is structurally unreachable on this type.
257:         """
258:         new_id = self._mint_group_id(empire)
259:         wing = FighterWing(
260:             group_id=new_id,
261:             owner_id=empire.id,
262:             location=target_hex,
263:             display_name=f"Fighter Wing {new_id}",
264:         )
265:         empire.deployed_groups.append(wing)
266:         return wing
267: 
268:     @staticmethod
269:     def _mint_group_id(empire: "Empire") -> int:
270:         existing = {
271:             g.id for g in empire.deployed_groups if isinstance(g.id, int)
272:         }
273:         candidate = 200000
274:         while candidate in existing:
275:             candidate += 1
276:         return candidate

138:         registries: Optional[Any],
139:     ) -> OrderExecutionResult:
140:         satellite_design_id = payload.get("satellite_design_id")
141:         count_raw = payload.get("count", 0)

144:         except (TypeError, ValueError):

156:         if len(popped) < count:
